# 第八轮手动整改完整执行合同

当前工作仅修订实现无关规格和必要追溯。用户另行手动启动后，按本文的唯一主批次完成29项必修，每项只计一次。10项optional及1项独立视觉policy均UNSELECTED，只有用户明确选择对应项才进入写集。状态UPDATE_NOT_STARTED；未来整改启动0。

固定B8 `fd1df570cad987dc3852a3b958f03916e317f7fe`，workspace tree `751dfde81048c81390f89a65ca1d1a565ba8e1ca`，formal tree `f292698cdd4a8e725525a9be6dc34b75faaf151e`。只读R `8c5911e4a4b07b07e832e4bb0d5d8859e88b4a9b` / tree `7589c800b61ba13a13040ed0d686979b80a84fd0`。项目 `/workspace/pokemon-essentials-clean-room`，参考 `/workspace/pokemon-essentials`，报告 `review/global-independent-review/eighth-2026-10-16-fd1df57-c84e`。

实际R8语义core commit `2460370f1e410bd49cc29e377b89662838aed2b3`，workspace tree `d43c425a3cfed29014f5cdfb94a3f8d947508d5f`，RR subtree `2e0487366deed90fdf34c64d47b372ff94530f10`，已普通commit/push并完整fresh远端VERIFIED。core-manifest为563500B/SHA256 d2650e97c5e8e3d2ed8f6c71de810fd7077036ebb1ef7cd5e5c31fa4dd09f7b8。C35之后生成的回读proof和root binding在input-payloads.json分别给精确path/mode/bytes/SHA/blob，其真实enclosing属于后继发布；它们不冒称C35成员。本新handoff enclosing commit/tree留null，root实际后继发布与外部绑定收口后核验，不能填未来hash。

全量合同语义审查及当前必要消费者/历史接收已完成：563固定源/正式文件=432R（44,906,485B）+131F（4,364,108B，111正文/17catalog/3shared）；87WP行=84内容+3阶段，18领域、113feature groups、395规划边、9crosscut。当前有效补充301载荷路径/289异SHA/274当前语义及15严格或混合用途处置，分母不可相加冒充统一atom。最终29主责任务、原9外部任务9/9全部root task-final接收；源/正式clause/必要consumer/history未读或未接收0。29逻辑primary不等于29实际context；已知累计125实际context=本地含root61+外部64，最终整合新增0。R7的42是唯一任务ID、12实际reviewer，U04/U05共享及续turn+0保持。

最终39是当前修订义务entries：29required（2P2、27P3）+10optional；独立14有限NO_REQUIRED、4binary负例、1视觉policy另列。UI003经GR7-M13-002 alias到R6-UI-PARTY-003；ELR为R6-D-LZ-002 parent上的新Slot consumer precision facet，保旧四family关闭；F05是在已知WP76-R12关闭事实上的未选WP58 attribution facet，保R6-DX-DEBUG-004/GIR-FD82-B029资格。这些不是39个新历史根。

行为证据分型严格保留：实际7个数学/有限证书过程和1个环境探针；旧PBS摘要1的原ID、I/O、exit、OS UTC仍UNVERIFIED/alias_UNRESOLVED，非阻塞且不另计第8数学过程。SOURCE_STATIC、DERIVED_VECTOR、TOOL_VALIDATION分开；参考Ruby/游戏/原生、真实保存回放、像素声音观察信用0。已有7+1的真实I/O及资格由coverage/final-execution-classification.json绑定；本handoff准备新增业务运行0。材料/宿主/opaque limits仍限原范围，可得未读不改标limit。语义审查完成不等于B8合规PASS，也不等于运行等价。

先完整读取AGENTS.md、inputs/review-SKILL.md、本文、同目录manifest、contracts.zh.json中所属ID的完整合同、所属完整readset及对应accepted-facts/history/policy伴件。按clean-document-revision直接形成正确最终文字；不抄源码、逐行转译、不开发新框架。原始完整根裁决和审计链按C35精确路径只读恢复。原33合同来源5caff的original commit/path与C35恢复路径双身份保留，不能把所有历史事实来源改成C35。

候选作者请求gpt-6.1-sol/xhigh，实际非作者审者请求gpt-6.1-sol/ultra，均default普速/Fast off；actual model/effort/tier/speed/Fast无权威echo逐项UNVERIFIED。原作者不能独立最终验收本人改动，角色名/同context续turn不产生独立性。每批作者只写其独占candidate mirror和证据；正式目标、共享整文件、planning/audit及公共台账仅R8-INTEGRATOR写。保留R、B8、main、原tag、旧报告、原接受/关闭回执和其它worktree。候选PASS、实际整合PASS、ACCEPTED、CLOSED四门分别登记；actual输入必须真实冻结，非作者完整核受影响整文件及累计保留，不能以候选PASS代替实际接收。

九批语义编辑DAG直接/传递边均0；共同发布/用户启动门满足后第一波9批可独立准备，实际并发服从工具slots，当前已启动0。共享写冲突通过整合单写者及逐贡献接收解决，不新增语义依赖。独立policy仅阻塞实际选择并依赖该policy的范围，未选optional不阻required关闭。

WP67-A由U06唯一候选writer接收原COM同步与faintaudio必修；CP01独立optional未选。F05仅用户选择时由U06候选writer改WP58 §5.1 L105相关短语，WP76/RC-W11正确只读，保RC29原ID/计数，当前WP58不进入必修candidate/actual写集。WP64与共享Creature catalog由U09候选贡献、R8-INTEGRATOR正式整文件唯一writer，完整保EG/EN/FC/TT/MG、Factory/Triad/E，32条计数/原ID/别名前缀。MG32精确输入为删空名字后RETURN并确认停止；邻近已有非空名ESCAPE成功不停止；不可把两者混同。

        "independent_findings": {
          "producer": "默认PBS第5行CATERPIE两努力项，经PBPokemon.fromInspected/createPokemon(50,31,合法训练家)各分510/2=255；Pokemon创建六EV0，模板后写255，calc_stats仅按ev/4读取，不规范化。",
          "order": "Stat.each_main通过Symbols.each按定义顺序，HP为第一主项；本例进入普通非Shadow分支时首个将处理HP。",
          "consumer": "pbGainEVsOne先读取EV产出副本/累计510/可用物品修正/病毒后进入逐主项；HP的单项剩余252−255=−3，原生整数clamp上下界0>−3先失败，未到总量夹限或本项写入。HP产出0与1均不能绕过这个区间有效性条件。",
          "default_gate": "pbOrganizedBattleEx在创建后明确internalBattle=false；普通pbGainExp先因!internalBattle退出，故正常设施不抵达该EV分配。没有必须expGain=false的证据，不应这样概括。",
          "formal": "WP19§5.3承认模板255却将增量模式写成通用min且没有反向区间失败。WP30§4.4描述clamp和先EV后经验但没有该输入失败。WP42§4.2仅引用数值并描述后序，同样不补这个输入门。",
          "contrary_formal": "WP28§6第6条与IU50已经限定“从受限状态增加”，这是正确保留范围，足以反驳“WP28无条件保证任何255输入安全”的广泛指控；不需要删除该限定或扩大本提案为全部物品外层错误。"
        },
        "finite_counterexample": {
          "domain": "Explicit call to already-defined EV allocation helper, or conditional ordinary internal battle growth consumer with valid parties/participant and internalBattle=true/expGain=true; no claim a default facility exports its rentals into normal play.",

    "3_前提反例及全部路径": {
      "contract": "玩家一健康、无伙伴、默认布局，[P HP0,Q健康]非蛋CATERPIE且无重写：布局1v1，实际席0/1/3；Q3有AI并能获命令，却不在全量priority0..1，普通/强制/Quash和增量不插入Q。野生早先peer/seen与后来的priority入场消费者分开。逆序[Q,P]使健康Q在1而入行动序；全健康两只为1v2且两者均入。3v1且玩家三健康可包括3，伙伴例可能更早owner位置失败，蛋混入先报活动蛋错误。正常场景返回是请求观察前提，不保证像素/胜利；部分建席/钩子失败不回滚。",

No form handler or external write alters HP/order. Prior resources/scenes/logging return normally; this is author-provided existing-object input, not a claim generated encounters routinely contain fainted members.",
          "ordinary_control": {

          },

              "AI wrappers are built for all existing seats0/1/3. The command phase scans all existing slots, skips faintedP, and reaches default enemy command for Q3. Wild ownership resolves trainer0; ordinary CATERPIE remains skill0, rejects switching, has no trainer items/Mega, and has valid positive move candidate weights.",
              "Full priority construction only considers configured indices0..1; its stored entries0/1 omitQ3. Living normal priority yields only0, while allBattlers yields0/3. Ordinary and forced/Quash move scans and incremental priority never insert the omittedQ.",
              "Conditional on normal command/scene returns and Q remaining healthy with UseMove, it is eligible for command but omitted from this ordinary action scan. No assertion of completed fight, final target outcome or eventual victory."

          },
          "consumer_reception": {
            "entry_effects": "pbOnAllBattlersEnteringBattle builds living index requests from allBattlers but actual per-member entry effects iterate speed priority; Q3 is therefore omitted there, despite earlier explicit wild peer hook/seen and later allBattlers form checks.",
            "ai": "Actual real receiver initializes AIBattler at3 and chooses ordinary move using the actual current battle; skill0 path/owner resolution read, not inferred merely from a method definition.",
            "scene": "Scene creates boxes/sprites for all existing slots and wild sprite changes for the full wild input. Single-side position/box functions have no offset lookup for sideSize1, so index3 is not intrinsically rejected there; target buttons and later intro data-box/cry passes use configured side sizes. This proves inconsistent static request sets, not pixels, visible overlap, sound or real animation success.",
            "judgment": "No immediate initial judge gate before commands. Normal later judgment uses participant-party able count, so livingQ in party prevents claiming an automatic victory merely because priority omits it."
          },

              "result": "REJECTED",

      "control_A": "专用_faint可解析：先形态后基础物种的同后缀查找；叫声默认音高100，ceil(1*20)+2=22。普通同时存在也不覆盖专用。",
      "counter_B": "仅专用缺、普通可解析：普通叫声请求音高75，长度入口同用75，ceil((1/0.75)*20)+2=29；一律100或未调整长度会得到22，满足旧概述仍错误。",
      "control_C": "专用/普通均无：不请求这两种species叫声，基础delay10+2=12；仍登记独立Pkmn faint音效，不说无任何音频。",
      "zero_and_resolution": "已选择资源但长度0的分支是0+2=2，不能合并成缺失12。专用查询本身只查同后缀；普通回退由外层另查；长度助手也有自身后缀回退，在稳定前提下本分支所选资源一致。",
      "ordering": "两条动画并发共同更新；成员在该delay处登记Pkmn faint并开始下落，框退场有自身轨道。全部轨道完成后聚合另次更新观察才完成；22/29/12不是总返回时长。",
      "cancel_failure_partial": "本轨道无选择取消/撤销濒死。场景可中止为外层异常/决定0路径；HP已先写battler+持久个体，展示后才提交败倒登记/状态幸福度/leave/choices。构造、更新、销毁无统一ensure回滚，不保证异常下框/成员后缀或领域正常清理。解析两资源缺失是正常分支，负音频长度钳0不证明宿主成功。",
      "evidence": "SOURCE_STATIC / DERIVED_VECTOR; all business, actual resource/audio/native outputs NOT_EXECUTED"
    },

      "opposition": "WP67-A只概述呈现阶段/阻塞；§9.4不转录逐帧轨迹/作者格式，WP74前向；媒体可替换，WP15与WP16已有共享规则，因此不必逐动画常量。",
      "accepted_part": "支持不要求所有颜色、轨迹、粒子或源码类/表布局；实际素材/发声/像素仍未知。",
      "resolution": "该反方不能为此入口选择75音高、专用优先、无资源10及额外2。§9.4面向招式/公共创作播放，WP74非目标排除战斗完整语义。有限sound request与依该请求计算等待属当前§9.3既有承诺，直接影响请求/释放先后，不需真实媒体/逐帧美术等价才能规定。因此有限P3 required成立。"

## 用户未来手动启动入口与日志

本环境实际成功入口为云平台collaboration.spawn_agent；launch-methods.json给作者xhigh/真实非作者ultra的完整arguments、逻辑ID与实际返回ID映射方法。每批的prompt.md与reviewer-prompt.md是独立完整入口，readset.json为完整读集。保存实际返回canonical task名/平台ID，使用collaboration.list_agents、send_message、wait_agent及平台任务卡查看可见状态；同context followup不增审者。每未来任务在独占目录记录progress、reading-ledger、commands.jsonl及真实stdout/stderr/exit/UTC、候选manifest；记录实际存在后可tail，不伪造私有平台流。

CLI备用仅语法已实际核验：codex-cli 0.159.0-alpha.3与exec --help均exit0；-C、-m、-s workspace-write、--json、-o、stdin“-”可见。原实际启动失败为readonly filesystem使in-process app-server client初始化失败，并非flag错误。完整argv、stdin及日志路径见launch-methods.json；help不证明授权、模型任务或业务成功，未核具体effort/tier配置key不编造。Desktop本云环境不可用。所有未来手动任务启动仍0。

云作者完整API模板（仅未来用户手动调用）：

```json
{
  "tool": "collaboration.spawn_agent",
  "arguments": {
    "task_name": "r8_a_u06",
    "fork_turns": "none",
    "model": "gpt-6.1-sol",
    "reasoning_effort": "xhigh",
    "message": "仅用户未来另行手动启动后执行。逻辑入口R8-A-R8-U06；项目/workspace/pokemon-essentials-clean-room，只读参考/workspace/pokemon-essentials。完整读取/workspace/pokemon-essentials-clean-room/review/global-independent-review/eighth-2026-10-16-fd1df57-c84e/handoff/batches/R8-U06/prompt.md及/workspace/pokemon-essentials-clean-room/review/global-independent-review/eighth-2026-10-16-fd1df57-c84e/handoff/cloud-eighth-update-handoff-manifest.json、/workspace/pokemon-essentials-clean-room/review/global-independent-review/eighth-2026-10-16-fd1df57-c84e/handoff/batches/R8-U06/readset.json与全部对应完整合同/必要伴件。固定语义core 2460370f1e410bd49cc29e377b89662838aed2b3，workspace tree d43c425a3cfed29014f5cdfb94a3f8d947508d5f，RR tree 2e0487366deed90fdf34c64d47b372ff94530f10；本handoff实际后继发布身份由外部真实绑定提供，不能编造。作者只在本批独占候选镜像/证据目录写；正式目标由R8-INTEGRATOR唯一写。F05未选不写WP58；WP67-A候选唯一writer处理COM同步与faintaudio，CP01未选不改。请求gpt-6.1-sol/xhigh/default normal/Fast off，实际无权威echo逐字段UNVERIFIED。另冻结实际候选/整合输入；作者不能最终接收本人改动，非作者完整核受影响全文/累计保留，四门分别登记。保存实际返回task ID与独立实读/进度/命令I/O/manifest。"
  }
}
```

CLI备用完整argv：

```json
[
  "codex",
  "exec",
  "-C",
  "/workspace/pokemon-essentials-clean-room",
  "-m",
  "gpt-6.1-sol",
  "-s",
  "workspace-write",
  "--json",
  "-o",
  "review/remediation-eighth-20261016-prepare/tasks/R8-A-R8-U06/last-message.txt",
  "-"
]
```

完整prompt由stdin提供，stdout JSONL、stderr、真实exit及last-message分别保存，具体路径见launch-methods.json。
